### 词群 14.01｜决定怎样旅行和去哪里 {#group-14-01}

**串联方式：** 先选旅行方式，再确定景点与纪念地，联系港口和签证准备。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">navigate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-001" aria-label="定位 navigate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-001" data-mark-label="navigate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="navigate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="navigate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 navigate" title="在有道查询 navigate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.航行；横渡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the voyage, we learned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>navigate</strong></a> and discovered how slow sea travel can turn a journey into an adventure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航行中，我们学习了导航，并发现缓慢的海上旅行如何把旅程变成冒险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Those who travel on this voyage must help <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>navigate</strong></a> during the final stage of the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参加这次航程的人必须在旅程最后阶段协助导航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The voyage follows an ancient travel route, and modern instruments help us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>navigate</strong></a> throughout the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航程沿着古代旅行路线展开，现代仪器帮助我们全程导航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">navigate a river</span> · 在河上航行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">navigation</a></span> · 导航</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expedition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">expedition</a></span> · 远征</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">voyage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-002" aria-label="定位 voyage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-002" data-mark-label="voyage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="voyage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="voyage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voyage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 voyage" title="在有道查询 voyage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.航海，航天；航行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voyage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>voyage</strong></a>, we learned to navigate and discovered how slow sea travel can turn a journey into an adventure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航行中，我们学习了导航，并发现缓慢的海上旅行如何把旅程变成冒险。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Those who travel on this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voyage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>voyage</strong></a> must help navigate during the final stage of the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参加这次航程的人必须在旅程最后阶段协助导航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=voyage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>voyage</strong></a> follows an ancient travel route, and modern instruments help us navigate throughout the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航程沿着古代旅行路线展开，现代仪器帮助我们全程导航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sea voyage</span> · 海上航行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=navigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">navigation</a></span> · 导航</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expedition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">expedition</a></span> · 远征</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-003">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.航空；航空学；航空工业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before flying on safari, check your visa and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aviation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aviation</strong></a> rules for small charter aircraft.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘飞机去观兽旅行前，检查签证及小型包机的航空规定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The safari company works with a local <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aviation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aviation</strong></a> operator and sends visitors visa guidance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">观兽旅行公司与本地航空运营商合作，并向游客发送签证指南。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aviation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aviation</strong></a> delay shortened our safari, but our visa remained valid for the revised dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空延误缩短了我们的观兽旅行，但签证在调整后的日期仍然有效。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">parade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-007" aria-label="定位 parade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-007" data-mark-label="parade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="parade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="parade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 parade" title="在有道查询 parade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.游行；炫耀；n.庆祝游行；列队行选；炫耀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The café is a favourite haunt for visitors watching the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parade</strong></a>, the town’s main attraction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家咖啡馆是观看游行的游客常去之处，游行是小镇的主要吸引点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A quiet local haunt became a tourist attraction after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parade</strong></a> route passed its door.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游行路线经过门前后，一处当地人常去的安静场所变成了旅游景点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The festival <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parade</strong></a> draws crowds to the square, an attraction that was once only a local haunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节庆游行把人群吸引到广场，这个景点从前只是当地人常去之处。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a street parade</span> · 街头游行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sightseeing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sightseeing</a></span> · 观光</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=procession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">procession</a></span> · 游行队伍</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-010">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tour leaves the port and visits a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorial</strong></a> before continuing to the pyramid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅行从港口出发，先参观纪念碑，再去金字塔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pyramid-shaped <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorial</strong></a> overlooks the old port.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一座金字塔形纪念碑俯瞰旧港口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the port, buses take visitors to the pyramid and the nearby <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorial</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公交车从港口把游客送到金字塔及附近纪念碑。</p></li>
</ol>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tour leaves the port and visits a memorial before continuing to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pyramid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pyramid</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅行从港口出发，先参观纪念碑，再去金字塔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pyramid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pyramid</strong></a>-shaped memorial overlooks the old port.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一座金字塔形纪念碑俯瞰旧港口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the port, buses take visitors to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pyramid&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pyramid</strong></a> and the nearby memorial.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公交车从港口把游客送到金字塔及附近纪念碑。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before flying on safari, check your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visa&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>visa</strong></a> and the aviation rules for small charter aircraft.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘飞机去观兽旅行前，检查签证及小型包机的航空规定。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An aviation delay shortened our safari, but our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visa&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>visa</strong></a> remained valid for the revised dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空延误缩短了我们的观兽旅行，但签证在调整后的日期仍然有效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">apply for a visa</span> · 申请签证</p>

</div>

### 词群 14.02｜搭乘飞机与托运行李 {#group-14-02}

**串联方式：** 认识航空公司、飞行器、飞行员和乘客，再整理行李、随身包及货运。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">traffic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-014" aria-label="定位 traffic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-014" data-mark-label="traffic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="traffic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="traffic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 traffic" title="在有道查询 traffic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.交通，车流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline’s pilot delayed the flight until air <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traffic</strong></a> cleared and the airplane could depart safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空公司的飞行员推迟了航班，直到空中交通畅通、飞机能安全起飞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The pilot reported heavy air <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traffic</strong></a>, so the airline kept the airplane at the gate before the flight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞行员报告空中交通繁忙，因此航空公司在起飞前把飞机留在登机口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline assigned an experienced pilot to the flight because the airplane would enter a busy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>traffic</strong></a> zone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">因为飞机将进入繁忙空域，航空公司给航班安排了有经验的飞行员。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">air traffic</span> · 空中交通</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=control%20tower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">control tower</a></span> · 塔台</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=departure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">departure</a></span> · 出发</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>airline</strong></a> assigned an experienced pilot to the flight because the airplane would enter a busy traffic zone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">因为飞机将进入繁忙空域，航空公司给航班安排了有经验的飞行员。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.直升机；v.用直升机载送；乘直升机旅行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The air show featured a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=helicopter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>helicopter</strong></a> rescue, a jet display and a parachute jump.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空展展示了直升机救援、喷气机表演和跳伞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=helicopter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>helicopter</strong></a> filmed the parachute team while a jet passed overhead.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">喷气机从头顶飞过时，一架直升机在拍摄跳伞队。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum displayed a rescue <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=helicopter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>helicopter</strong></a> beside a jet engine and an emergency parachute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆在喷气发动机和应急降落伞旁展出了一架救援直升机。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pilot</strong></a> delayed the flight until air traffic cleared and the airplane could depart safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空公司的飞行员推迟了航班，直到空中交通畅通、飞机能安全起飞。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pilot</strong></a> reported heavy air traffic, so the airline kept the airplane at the gate before the flight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞行员报告空中交通繁忙，因此航空公司在起飞前把飞机留在登机口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline assigned an experienced <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pilot</strong></a> to the flight because the airplane would enter a busy traffic zone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">因为飞机将进入繁忙空域，航空公司给航班安排了有经验的飞行员。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an experienced pilot</span> · 有经验的飞行员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=control%20tower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">control tower</a></span> · 塔台</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=departure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">departure</a></span> · 出发</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">passenger<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-022" aria-label="定位 passenger">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-022" data-mark-label="passenger" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="passenger：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="passenger：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 passenger" title="在有道查询 passenger">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.乘客</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passenger</strong></a> may check one suitcase as baggage and take a small carry-on bag as hand luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位乘客可以托运一个行李箱，并携带一个小登机包作为手提行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passenger</strong></a> placed her suitcase at the baggage desk and kept her carry-on luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客把行李箱放在托运柜台，留下随身行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carry-on suitcase counts towards each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passenger</strong></a>’s hand luggage allowance, separate from checked baggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登机行李箱计入每位乘客的手提行李限额，与托运行李另算。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a passenger seat</span> · 乘客座位</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=check-in&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">check-in</a></span> · 值机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allowance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allowance</a></span> · 限额</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-023">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-023" class="vocabulary-word-title">baggage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-023" aria-label="定位 baggage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-023" data-mark-label="baggage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="baggage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="baggage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 baggage" title="在有道查询 baggage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-023</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. ［尤美］行李</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each passenger may check one suitcase as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baggage</strong></a> and take a small carry-on bag as hand luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位乘客可以托运一个行李箱，并携带一个小登机包作为手提行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passenger placed her suitcase at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baggage</strong></a> desk and kept her carry-on luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客把行李箱放在托运柜台，留下随身行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carry-on suitcase counts towards each passenger’s hand luggage allowance, separate from checked <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baggage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登机行李箱计入每位乘客的手提行李限额，与托运行李另算。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">checked baggage</span> · 托运行李</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=check-in&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">check-in</a></span> · 值机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allowance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allowance</a></span> · 限额</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-024">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-024" class="vocabulary-word-title">luggage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-024" aria-label="定位 luggage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-024" data-mark-label="luggage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="luggage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="luggage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 luggage" title="在有道查询 luggage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-024</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passenger placed her suitcase at the baggage desk and kept her carry-on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>luggage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客把行李箱放在托运柜台，留下随身行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carry-on suitcase counts towards each passenger’s hand <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luggage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>luggage</strong></a> allowance, separate from checked baggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登机行李箱计入每位乘客的手提行李限额，与托运行李另算。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hand luggage</span> · 手提行李</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=check-in&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">check-in</a></span> · 值机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allowance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allowance</a></span> · 限额</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-025">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.手提箱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each passenger may check one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitcase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitcase</strong></a> as baggage and take a small carry-on bag as hand luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位乘客可以托运一个行李箱，并携带一个小登机包作为手提行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passenger placed her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitcase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitcase</strong></a> at the baggage desk and kept her carry-on luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客把行李箱放在托运柜台，留下随身行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carry-on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitcase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitcase</strong></a> counts towards each passenger’s hand luggage allowance, separate from checked baggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登机行李箱计入每位乘客的手提行李限额，与托运行李另算。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pack a suitcase</span> · 收拾行李箱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=check-in&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">check-in</a></span> · 值机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allowance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allowance</a></span> · 限额</li></ul>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.［美］(可随身携带上飞机的)小包；adj.随身携带上飞机的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each passenger may check one suitcase as baggage and take a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carry-on&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carry-on</strong></a> bag as hand luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位乘客可以托运一个行李箱，并携带一个小登机包作为手提行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passenger placed her suitcase at the baggage desk and kept her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carry-on&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carry-on</strong></a> luggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客把行李箱放在托运柜台，留下随身行李。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carry-on&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carry-on</strong></a> suitcase counts towards each passenger’s hand luggage allowance, separate from checked baggage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登机行李箱计入每位乘客的手提行李限额，与托运行李另算。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">rack<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-027" aria-label="定位 rack">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-027" data-mark-label="rack" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rack：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rack：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rack" title="在有道查询 rack">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n搁物架，挂物架</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The freight compartment has a reinforced <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rack</strong></a> for fragile boxes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">货舱有加固架，用来放易碎货箱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Secure the freight on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rack</strong></a> before take-off.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起飞前把货物固定在架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline added a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rack</strong></a> to improve freight storage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空公司增设架子，改善货物存放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a luggage rack</span> · 行李架</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cargo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cargo</a></span> · 货物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loading&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">loading</a></span> · 装载</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">freight<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-028" aria-label="定位 freight">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-028" data-mark-label="freight" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="freight：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="freight：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=freight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 freight" title="在有道查询 freight">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.货运；货物；v.运送（货物)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=freight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>freight</strong></a> compartment has a reinforced rack for fragile boxes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">货舱有加固架，用来放易碎货箱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Secure the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=freight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>freight</strong></a> on the rack before take-off.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起飞前把货物固定在架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The airline added a new rack to improve <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=freight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>freight</strong></a> storage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">航空公司增设架子，改善货物存放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">air freight</span> · 航空货运</p>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 14.03｜规划路线并辨认道路 {#group-14-03}

**串联方式：** 用地图集、票价、线路和行程做计划，再沿路口、车道、大道与公路看路标。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">atlas<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-029" aria-label="定位 atlas">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-029" data-mark-label="atlas" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="atlas：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="atlas：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atlas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 atlas" title="在有道查询 atlas">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-029</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our itinerary follows a scenic route from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atlas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>atlas</strong></a>, and the bus fare is included.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的行程沿地图册上的风景路线展开，已含公交票价。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The travel desk supplied an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atlas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>atlas</strong></a>, a route map, a fare list and a suggested itinerary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅游咨询台提供地图册、路线图、票价表和建议行程。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">passage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-033" aria-label="定位 passage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-033" data-mark-label="passage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="passage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="passage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 passage" title="在有道查询 passage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.通过；通道，走廊</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Follow the path through the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passage</strong></a>, then cross at the intersection to find your way to the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">沿小径穿过通道，再在路口过马路，找到去车站的路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passage</strong></a> is the quickest way to the intersection where you can cross onto the riverside path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通道是去路口最快的路，从路口可以穿过去上河边小径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We lost our way after leaving the path and had to cross the intersection to reach a narrow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=passage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>passage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开小径后我们迷了路，不得不穿过路口去一条窄通道。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a narrow passage</span> · 狭窄通道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crossing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crossing</a></span> · 过街处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedestrian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pedestrian</a></span> · 行人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Follow the path through the passage, then cross at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intersection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intersection</strong></a> to find your way to the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">沿小径穿过通道，再在路口过马路，找到去车站的路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passage is the quickest way to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intersection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intersection</strong></a> where you can cross onto the riverside path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通道是去路口最快的路，从路口可以穿过去上河边小径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We lost our way after leaving the path and had to cross the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intersection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intersection</strong></a> to reach a narrow passage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开小径后我们迷了路，不得不穿过路口去一条窄通道。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a busy intersection</span> · 繁忙路口</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-035">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Follow the path through the passage, then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cross&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cross</strong></a> at the intersection to find your way to the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">沿小径穿过通道，再在路口过马路，找到去车站的路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passage is the quickest way to the intersection where you can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cross&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cross</strong></a> onto the riverside path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通道是去路口最快的路，从路口可以穿过去上河边小径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We lost our way after leaving the path and had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cross&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cross</strong></a> the intersection to reach a narrow passage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开小径后我们迷了路，不得不穿过路口去一条窄通道。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Follow the path through the passage, then cross at the intersection to find your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=way&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>way</strong></a> to the station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">沿小径穿过通道，再在路口过马路，找到去车站的路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passage is the quickest <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=way&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>way</strong></a> to the intersection where you can cross onto the riverside path.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通道是去路口最快的路，从路口可以穿过去上河边小径。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We lost our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=way&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>way</strong></a> after leaving the path and had to cross the intersection to reach a narrow passage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开小径后我们迷了路，不得不穿过路口去一条窄通道。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The passage is the quickest way to the intersection where you can cross onto the riverside <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=path&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>path</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通道是去路口最快的路，从路口可以穿过去上河边小径。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-038">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-038" class="vocabulary-word-title">lane<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-038" aria-label="定位 lane">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-038" data-mark-label="lane" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lane：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lane：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lane" title="在有道查询 lane">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-038</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.小巷；车道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A signpost by the curb directs cyclists from the avenue into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lane</strong></a> beside the highway.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">路缘旁的路标指引骑行者从大道进入公路旁的一条车道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The narrow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lane</strong></a> joins the avenue near a signpost, where a curb separates it from the highway entrance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窄车道在路标附近汇入大道，那里有路缘将其与公路入口隔开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan adds a cycle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lane</strong></a> along the avenue and a signpost near the highway, with a protective curb between cyclists and cars.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">方案在大道上增设自行车道，在公路旁增设路标，并用防护路缘隔开自行车和汽车。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cycle lane</span> · 自行车道</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=junction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">junction</a></span> · 交汇处</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=road%20markings&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">road markings</a></span> · 道路标线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The narrow lane joins the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=avenue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>avenue</strong></a> near a signpost, where a curb separates it from the highway entrance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窄车道在路标附近汇入大道，那里有路缘将其与公路入口隔开。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.路标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signpost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>signpost</strong></a> by the curb directs cyclists from the avenue into a lane beside the highway.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">路缘旁的路标指引骑行者从大道进入公路旁的一条车道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The narrow lane joins the avenue near a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signpost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>signpost</strong></a>, where a curb separates it from the highway entrance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窄车道在路标附近汇入大道，那里有路缘将其与公路入口隔开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan adds a cycle lane along the avenue and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signpost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>signpost</strong></a> near the highway, with a protective curb between cyclists and cars.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">方案在大道上增设自行车道，在公路旁增设路标，并用防护路缘隔开自行车和汽车。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pave</strong></a> the muddy road before winter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政将在冬天前铺好泥泞道路。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A smooth surface will make the route easier to use once they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pave</strong></a> it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们铺好路线后，平整路面将使通行更方便。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 14.04｜选择陆路交通工具 {#group-14-04}

**串联方式：** 比较汽车、公共交通、货运、农业及人力交通工具；express 可指快车或快运。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-044">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The transport display compares a private <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vehicle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vehicle</strong></a> from the auto industry with a tram, a coach and an express train.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">交通展把汽车业生产的私家车与电车、长途客车和特快列车作比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Auto use declined as commuters left their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vehicle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vehicle</strong></a> at home and chose a tram, coach or express service.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通勤者把车留在家里，选择电车、长途客车或特快服务后，汽车使用减少了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The auto exhibition includes an electric <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vehicle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vehicle</strong></a>, while the nearby station connects the tram, coach and express train.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">汽车展有电动车，附近车站则衔接电车、长途客车和特快列车。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The depot has a van and a small truck, while the nearby hospital parks an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambulance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ambulance</strong></a> beside a supply lorry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车站有厢式车和小卡车，附近医院则把救护车停在补给卡车旁。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">truck<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-050" aria-label="定位 truck">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-050" data-mark-label="truck" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="truck：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="truck：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=truck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 truck" title="在有道查询 truck">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 卡车；敞篷货运火车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The ambulance passed a delivery van and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=truck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>truck</strong></a> before overtaking a slow lorry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救护车经过送货厢式车和卡车，再超过一辆慢行重卡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lorry blocked the van, so the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=truck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>truck</strong></a> driver moved aside to let the ambulance through.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重卡挡住了厢式车，因此卡车司机让到一边，让救护车通过。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The depot has a van and a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=truck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>truck</strong></a>, while the nearby hospital parks an ambulance beside a supply lorry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车站有厢式车和小卡车，附近医院则把救护车停在补给卡车旁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a delivery truck</span> · 送货卡车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency%20lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emergency lane</a></span> · 应急车道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delivery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">delivery</a></span> · 配送</li></ul>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lorry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lorry</strong></a> blocked the van, so the truck driver moved aside to let the ambulance through.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重卡挡住了厢式车，因此卡车司机让到一边，让救护车通过。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">wagon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-053" aria-label="定位 wagon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-053" data-mark-label="wagon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wagon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wagon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wagon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wagon" title="在有道查询 wagon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 四轮运货马车(或牛车)；［英］(火车的)货车车厢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum shows a freight <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wagon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wagon</strong></a>, a passenger carriage, a tractor and a horse-drawn cart.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆展出货运车厢、客车车厢、拖拉机和马车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tractor pulled the cart past an old railway carriage and a rusting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wagon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wagon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">拖拉机拉着小车，驶过旧客车车厢和生锈货车车厢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm replaced its cart with a tractor, while the railway replaced its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wagon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wagon</strong></a> and carriage with modern stock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场用拖拉机取代马车，铁路则用现代车辆替换旧货车和客车车厢。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a freight wagon</span> · 货运车厢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rolling%20stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rolling stock</a></span> · 铁路车辆</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agricultural%20machinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">agricultural machinery</a></span> · 农业机械</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm replaced its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cart&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cart</strong></a> with a tractor, while the railway replaced its wagon and carriage with modern stock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场用拖拉机取代马车，铁路则用现代车辆替换旧货车和客车车厢。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a horse-drawn cart</span> · 马车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rolling%20stock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rolling stock</a></span> · 铁路车辆</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agricultural%20machinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">agricultural machinery</a></span> · 农业机械</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">pedicab<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-057" aria-label="定位 pedicab">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-057" data-mark-label="pedicab" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pedicab：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pedicab：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedicab&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pedicab" title="在有道查询 pedicab">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n(人力)三轮车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can cycle around the old town or take a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedicab&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedicab</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以骑车游览老城，也可以坐人力三轮车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedicab&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedicab</strong></a> driver must cycle slowly through crowded streets.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人力三轮车司机必须在拥挤街道慢慢骑行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tourists who cannot cycle can still enjoy the route in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedicab&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedicab</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不会骑车的游客仍可乘三轮车欣赏这条路线。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a pedicab ride</span> · 三轮车之旅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rickshaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rickshaw</a></span> · 人力车</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling%20lane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cycling lane</a></span> · 自行车道</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-058">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cycle</strong></a> around the old town or take a pedicab.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以骑车游览老城，也可以坐人力三轮车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The pedicab driver must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cycle</strong></a> slowly through crowded streets.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人力三轮车司机必须在拥挤街道慢慢骑行。</p></li>

</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=garage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>garage</strong></a> tested the motor and confirmed its advertised horsepower.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修理厂测试了发动机，确认其马力符合宣传。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">wheel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-062" aria-label="定位 wheel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-062" data-mark-label="wheel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wheel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wheel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wheel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wheel" title="在有道查询 wheel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-062</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.方向盘；车轮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hold the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wheel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wheel</strong></a>, press the brake and honk if necessary when a damaged tyre makes the car unstable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轮胎损坏使车不稳时，握住方向盘、踩刹车，必要时鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The driving test checks <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wheel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wheel</strong></a> control, brake use and tyre awareness, including when it is appropriate to honk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">驾驶考试检查方向盘控制、刹车使用和轮胎安全意识，也包括何时适合鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose tyre on the road forced the driver to grip the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wheel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wheel</strong></a>, brake and honk at approaching traffic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">道路上一条松脱的轮胎迫使司机握紧方向盘、刹车，并向来车鸣笛。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a steering wheel</span> · 方向盘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=road%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">road safety</a></span> · 道路安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">horn</a></span> · 喇叭</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-063">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 车闸；刹车；v. 刹车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hold the wheel, press the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brake</strong></a> and honk if necessary when a damaged tyre makes the car unstable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轮胎损坏使车不稳时，握住方向盘、踩刹车，必要时鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The driving test checks wheel control, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brake</strong></a> use and tyre awareness, including when it is appropriate to honk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">驾驶考试检查方向盘控制、刹车使用和轮胎安全意识，也包括何时适合鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose tyre on the road forced the driver to grip the wheel, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brake</strong></a> and honk at approaching traffic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">道路上一条松脱的轮胎迫使司机握紧方向盘、刹车，并向来车鸣笛。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">apply the brake</span> · 踩刹车</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">tyre<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-064" aria-label="定位 tyre">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-064" data-mark-label="tyre" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tyre：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tyre：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tyre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tyre" title="在有道查询 tyre">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(=tire)；n.轮胎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hold the wheel, press the brake and honk if necessary when a damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tyre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tyre</strong></a> makes the car unstable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轮胎损坏使车不稳时，握住方向盘、踩刹车，必要时鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The driving test checks wheel control, brake use and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tyre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tyre</strong></a> awareness, including when it is appropriate to honk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">驾驶考试检查方向盘控制、刹车使用和轮胎安全意识，也包括何时适合鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tyre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tyre</strong></a> on the road forced the driver to grip the wheel, brake and honk at approaching traffic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">道路上一条松脱的轮胎迫使司机握紧方向盘、刹车，并向来车鸣笛。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a flat tyre</span> · 瘪轮胎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=road%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">road safety</a></span> · 道路安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">horn</a></span> · 喇叭</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-065">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 汽车喇叭声；鹅叫声；v. 按(汽车喇叭)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Hold the wheel, press the brake and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honk</strong></a> if necessary when a damaged tyre makes the car unstable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轮胎损坏使车不稳时，握住方向盘、踩刹车，必要时鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The driving test checks wheel control, brake use and tyre awareness, including when it is appropriate to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">驾驶考试检查方向盘控制、刹车使用和轮胎安全意识，也包括何时适合鸣笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose tyre on the road forced the driver to grip the wheel, brake and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honk</strong></a> at approaching traffic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">道路上一条松脱的轮胎迫使司机握紧方向盘、刹车，并向来车鸣笛。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 14.06｜旅途中的工作人员与险情 {#group-14-06}

**串联方式：** 先认机组、船长或乘务，再看船尾、包层和事故；用紧急、撞击与危险解释险情。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">crew<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-066" aria-label="定位 crew">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-066" data-mark-label="crew" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="crew：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="crew：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 crew" title="在有道查询 crew">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (轮船、飞机等上的)全体工作人员；(有专门技术的) 一组工作人员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The captain sent a steward to join the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crew</strong></a> at the stern.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船长派一名乘务员到船尾与船员会合。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A steward guided passengers away from the stern while the captain briefed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crew</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船长向船员说明情况时，一名乘务员引导乘客远离船尾。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crew</strong></a> gathered at the stern, where the captain thanked the steward for helping passengers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员聚在船尾，船长在那里感谢乘务员帮助乘客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a ship’s crew</span> · 船员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bridge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bridge</a></span> · 驾驶台</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lifeboat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lifeboat</a></span> · 救生艇</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">envelope<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-070" aria-label="定位 envelope">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-070" data-mark-label="envelope" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="envelope：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="envelope：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=envelope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 envelope" title="在有道查询 envelope">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 信封；包层；气囊外壳（航空相关语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The balloon’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=envelope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>envelope</strong></a> expanded as hot air filled it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">热空气充入时，气球气囊逐渐膨胀。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=envelope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>envelope</strong></a> for tears before inflating the balloon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">给气球充气前，检查气囊有无破口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The colourful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=envelope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>envelope</strong></a> was spread across the field before take-off.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起飞前，彩色气囊铺展在田地上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a balloon envelope</span> · 气球气囊</p>
