<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">emergency<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-071" aria-label="定位 emergency">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-071" data-mark-label="emergency" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="emergency：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="emergency：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 emergency" title="在有道查询 emergency">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.紧急事件；adj.应急的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crash left a wreck that posed a hazard, so the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emergency</strong></a> team checked that no loose panel could crush a rescuer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">碰撞留下的残骸构成危险，应急队于是检查了是否有松动面板可能砸伤救援者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emergency</strong></a>, a crash can turn a vehicle into a wreck and leave a hazard that may crush nearby equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧急情况下，碰撞会把车变成残骸，并留下可能压坏附近设备的隐患。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emergency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emergency</strong></a> report warned that unstable parts of the crash wreck were a hazard because they could crush workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急报告警告，事故残骸的不稳定部件可能压伤工人，因此十分危险。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an emergency response</span> · 应急响应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debris&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">debris</a></span> · 残骸碎片</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">wreck<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-072" aria-label="定位 wreck">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-072" data-mark-label="wreck" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wreck：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wreck：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wreck" title="在有道查询 wreck">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使失事；破坏；n.失事的船只(或汽车、飞机等)；残破的东西</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crash left a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreck</strong></a> that posed a hazard, so the emergency team checked that no loose panel could crush a rescuer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">碰撞留下的残骸构成危险，应急队于是检查了是否有松动面板可能砸伤救援者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In an emergency, a crash can turn a vehicle into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreck</strong></a> and leave a hazard that may crush nearby equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧急情况下，碰撞会把车变成残骸，并留下可能压坏附近设备的隐患。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The emergency report warned that unstable parts of the crash <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreck</strong></a> were a hazard because they could crush workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急报告警告，事故残骸的不稳定部件可能压伤工人，因此十分危险。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rusting wreck</span> · 生锈的残骸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debris&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">debris</a></span> · 残骸碎片</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-073">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crash</strong></a> left a wreck that posed a hazard, so the emergency team checked that no loose panel could crush a rescuer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">碰撞留下的残骸构成危险，应急队于是检查了是否有松动面板可能砸伤救援者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In an emergency, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crash</strong></a> can turn a vehicle into a wreck and leave a hazard that may crush nearby equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧急情况下，碰撞会把车变成残骸，并留下可能压坏附近设备的隐患。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The emergency report warned that unstable parts of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crash</strong></a> wreck were a hazard because they could crush workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急报告警告，事故残骸的不稳定部件可能压伤工人，因此十分危险。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a plane crash</span> · 坠机事故</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debris&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">debris</a></span> · 残骸碎片</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In an emergency, a crash can turn a vehicle into a wreck and leave a hazard that may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crush&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crush</strong></a> nearby equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧急情况下，碰撞会把车变成残骸，并留下可能压坏附近设备的隐患。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-075">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-075" class="vocabulary-word-title">dash<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-075" aria-label="定位 dash">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-075" data-mark-label="dash" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dash：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dash：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dash" title="在有道查询 dash">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-075</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 猛冲；猛摔</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the runners <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dash</strong></a> into the tunnel, disappear around a bend and vanish from sight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着跑者冲进隧道、消失在弯道后，完全不见了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dash</strong></a> across the road when vehicles can disappear behind parked vans and vanish from view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车辆可能消失在停着的厢式车后、离开视线时，不要冲过马路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sudden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dash</strong></a> towards the station made her disappear into the crowd and vanish from the camera’s view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她突然冲向车站，隐入人群，从镜头中消失了。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dash for shelter</span> · 奔向避难处</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hurry</a></span> · 匆忙</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">vanish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-076" aria-label="定位 vanish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-076" data-mark-label="vanish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vanish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vanish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vanish" title="在有道查询 vanish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 突然消失；绝迹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the runners dash into the tunnel, disappear around a bend and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vanish</strong></a> from sight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着跑者冲进隧道、消失在弯道后，完全不见了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not dash across the road when vehicles can disappear behind parked vans and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vanish</strong></a> from view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车辆可能消失在停着的厢式车后、离开视线时，不要冲过马路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sudden dash towards the station made her disappear into the crowd and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vanish</strong></a> from the camera’s view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她突然冲向车站，隐入人群，从镜头中消失了。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">vanish from sight</span> · 从视野消失</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hurry</a></span> · 匆忙</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">disappear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-077" aria-label="定位 disappear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-077" data-mark-label="disappear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="disappear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="disappear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disappear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 disappear" title="在有道查询 disappear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 失踪；消失</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the runners dash into the tunnel, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disappear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disappear</strong></a> around a bend and vanish from sight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着跑者冲进隧道、消失在弯道后，完全不见了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not dash across the road when vehicles can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disappear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disappear</strong></a> behind parked vans and vanish from view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车辆可能消失在停着的厢式车后、离开视线时，不要冲过马路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sudden dash towards the station made her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disappear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disappear</strong></a> into the crowd and vanish from the camera’s view.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她突然冲向车站，隐入人群，从镜头中消失了。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">disappear into fog</span> · 消失在雾中</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visibility</a></span> · 能见度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hurry</a></span> · 匆忙</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">hazard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-078" aria-label="定位 hazard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-078" data-mark-label="hazard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hazard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hazard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hazard" title="在有道查询 hazard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 危险；v. 使冒风险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crash left a wreck that posed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hazard</strong></a>, so the emergency team checked that no loose panel could crush a rescuer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">碰撞留下的残骸构成危险，应急队于是检查了是否有松动面板可能砸伤救援者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In an emergency, a crash can turn a vehicle into a wreck and leave a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hazard</strong></a> that may crush nearby equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧急情况下，碰撞会把车变成残骸，并留下可能压坏附近设备的隐患。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The emergency report warned that unstable parts of the crash wreck were a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hazard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hazard</strong></a> because they could crush workers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急报告警告，事故残骸的不稳定部件可能压伤工人，因此十分危险。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a traffic hazard</span> · 交通隐患</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rescue</a></span> · 救援</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=debris&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">debris</a></span> · 残骸碎片</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 14.07｜从港口上船出航 {#group-14-07}

**串联方式：** 到港停靠并下锚，认识船员和船型，再看船体设备与登船动作。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">harbour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-079" aria-label="定位 harbour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-079" data-mark-label="harbour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="harbour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="harbour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harbour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 harbour" title="在有道查询 harbour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">( = harbor)；n.港口</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Drop the anchor near the dock before waves submerge the lower part of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harbour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harbour</strong></a> wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">浪淹没港墙下部前，在码头附近抛锚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">High tides can submerge the old dock, so boats anchor farther inside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harbour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harbour</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高潮会淹没旧码头，因此船在港口更里面抛锚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harbour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harbour</strong></a> master watched the anchor submerge as the vessel stopped short of the dock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船在码头前停住时，港务长看着锚沉入水中。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sheltered harbour</span> · 避风港</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mooring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mooring</a></span> · 系泊</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tide</a></span> · 潮汐</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-083">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sailor</strong></a> had served as a seaman on a steamer and a liner before joining the carrier.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这名水手加入航母前，曾在汽船和远洋客轮当海员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A retired seaman told the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sailor</strong></a> about life aboard a steamer, a liner and an aircraft carrier.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">退休海员向水手讲述了汽船、远洋客轮和航母上的生活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum interviewed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sailor</strong></a> from the carrier and a merchant seaman who had worked on both a steamer and a liner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆采访了一位航母水手和一位曾在汽船、远洋客轮工作的商船海员。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-088">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.大船，舰；容器，器皿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers embark on the ferry and stay aboard the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vessel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vessel</strong></a> as we sail across the bay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客登上渡船，在我们驶过海湾期间留在船上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you embark, check the ferry schedule and ask when the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vessel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vessel</strong></a> will sail with everyone aboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登船前查看渡船时刻表，并询问所有人上船后船何时启航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew helped families embark on the ferry, then prepared to sail once every passenger was aboard the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vessel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vessel</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员帮助家庭登上渡船，等每位乘客都在船上后便准备启航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cargo vessel</span> · 货船</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boarding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">boarding</a></span> · 登船</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crossing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crossing</a></span> · 渡航</li></ul>
</div>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers embark on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ferry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ferry</strong></a> and stay aboard the vessel as we sail across the bay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客登上渡船，在我们驶过海湾期间留在船上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you embark, check the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ferry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ferry</strong></a> schedule and ask when the vessel will sail with everyone aboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登船前查看渡船时刻表，并询问所有人上船后船何时启航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew helped families embark on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ferry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ferry</strong></a>, then prepared to sail once every passenger was aboard the vessel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员帮助家庭登上渡船，等每位乘客都在船上后便准备启航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a passenger ferry</span> · 客渡船</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boarding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">boarding</a></span> · 登船</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crossing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crossing</a></span> · 渡航</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers embark on the ferry and stay aboard the vessel as we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sail</strong></a> across the bay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客登上渡船，在我们驶过海湾期间留在船上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you embark, check the ferry schedule and ask when the vessel will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sail</strong></a> with everyone aboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登船前查看渡船时刻表，并询问所有人上船后船何时启航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew helped families embark on the ferry, then prepared to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sail</strong></a> once every passenger was aboard the vessel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员帮助家庭登上渡船，等每位乘客都在船上后便准备启航。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">raft<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-091" aria-label="定位 raft">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-091" data-mark-label="raft" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="raft：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="raft：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 raft" title="在有道查询 raft">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 木筏，木排</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the deck, we saw an oar float between a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raft</strong></a> and a canoe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从甲板上，我们看到一支桨漂在木筏和独木舟之间。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The canoe and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raft</strong></a> were secured on deck beside a spare oar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独木舟和木筏固定在甲板上，旁边放着备用桨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sailor lowered the canoe from the deck while another pushed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raft</strong></a> away with an oar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名水手从甲板放下独木舟，另一名用桨推开木筏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wooden raft</span> · 木筏</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">canoe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-092" aria-label="定位 canoe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-092" data-mark-label="canoe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="canoe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="canoe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canoe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 canoe" title="在有道查询 canoe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.独木舟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From the deck, we saw an oar float between a raft and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canoe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>canoe</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从甲板上，我们看到一支桨漂在木筏和独木舟之间。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canoe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>canoe</strong></a> and raft were secured on deck beside a spare oar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独木舟和木筏固定在甲板上，旁边放着备用桨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sailor lowered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canoe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>canoe</strong></a> from the deck while another pushed the raft away with an oar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名水手从甲板放下独木舟，另一名用桨推开木筏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">paddle a canoe</span> · 划独木舟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=paddle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">paddle</a></span> · 短桨</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">turbine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-095" aria-label="定位 turbine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-095" data-mark-label="turbine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="turbine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="turbine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turbine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 turbine" title="在有道查询 turbine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 涡轮机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turbine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turbine</strong></a> drives the ship’s propeller through a gearbox.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涡轮机通过齿轮箱驱动船的螺旋桨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers inspected the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turbine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turbine</strong></a> before testing the propeller.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师测试螺旋桨前，先检查了涡轮机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A more efficient <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turbine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turbine</strong></a> reduced fuel use without changing the propeller.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更高效的涡轮机在无需更换螺旋桨的情况下减少了燃料消耗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a steam turbine</span> · 蒸汽涡轮机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propulsion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">propulsion</a></span> · 推进</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shaft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shaft</a></span> · 轴</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">propeller<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-096" aria-label="定位 propeller">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-096" data-mark-label="propeller" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="propeller：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="propeller：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propeller&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 propeller" title="在有道查询 propeller">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-096</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The turbine drives the ship’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propeller&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propeller</strong></a> through a gearbox.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">涡轮机通过齿轮箱驱动船的螺旋桨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers inspected the turbine before testing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propeller&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propeller</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师测试螺旋桨前，先检查了涡轮机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A more efficient turbine reduced fuel use without changing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propeller&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propeller</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更高效的涡轮机在无需更换螺旋桨的情况下减少了燃料消耗。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">aboard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-097" aria-label="定位 aboard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-097" data-mark-label="aboard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="aboard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="aboard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 aboard" title="在有道查询 aboard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adv.在(船、飞机等)上</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers embark on the ferry and stay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aboard</strong></a> the vessel as we sail across the bay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客登上渡船，在我们驶过海湾期间留在船上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you embark, check the ferry schedule and ask when the vessel will sail with everyone <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aboard</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登船前查看渡船时刻表，并询问所有人上船后船何时启航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew helped families embark on the ferry, then prepared to sail once every passenger was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aboard</strong></a> the vessel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员帮助家庭登上渡船，等每位乘客都在船上后便准备启航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">come aboard</span> · 登船</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">embark<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-098" aria-label="定位 embark">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-098" data-mark-label="embark" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="embark：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="embark：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 embark" title="在有道查询 embark">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embark</strong></a> on the ferry and stay aboard the vessel as we sail across the bay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客登上渡船，在我们驶过海湾期间留在船上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embark</strong></a>, check the ferry schedule and ask when the vessel will sail with everyone aboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登船前查看渡船时刻表，并询问所有人上船后船何时启航。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew helped families <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embark</strong></a> on the ferry, then prepared to sail once every passenger was aboard the vessel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">船员帮助家庭登上渡船，等每位乘客都在船上后便准备启航。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">embark on a ship</span> · 登船</p>

### 词群 14.08｜水路与铁路通道 {#group-14-08}

**串联方式：** 先看海峡、运河和沟渠，再切换铁路、机车、地铁、隧道与轨道。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">channel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-099" aria-label="定位 channel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-099" data-mark-label="channel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="channel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="channel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=channel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 channel" title="在有道查询 channel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 航道；电视频道；海峡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A drainage ditch feeds the canal beside the main shipping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=channel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>channel</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">排水沟流入主航道旁的运河。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.［美］铁路，铁路公司</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=railroad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>railroad</strong></a> company restored a steam locomotive and replaced a damaged rail on the heritage railway.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">铁路公司修复了一台蒸汽机车，并更换了观光铁路上的一根损坏钢轨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The railway museum displays a locomotive, a section of rail and an old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=railroad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>railroad</strong></a> company sign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">铁路博物馆展出机车、一段钢轨和一块旧铁路公司标牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken rail stopped the locomotive, forcing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=railroad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>railroad</strong></a> operator to close the railway temporarily.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一根断轨使机车停下，迫使铁路运营商暂时关闭铁路。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a railroad company</span> · 铁路公司</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subway</strong></a> runs through an underground tunnel beneath the river.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地铁穿过河下的地下隧道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers closed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subway</strong></a> tunnel to inspect the underground structure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师关闭地铁隧道，检查地下结构。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new underground tunnel connects two <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=subway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>subway</strong></a> lines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新地下隧道连接两条地铁线路。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The subway runs through an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=underground&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>underground</strong></a> tunnel beneath the river.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地铁穿过河下的地下隧道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers closed the subway tunnel to inspect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=underground&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>underground</strong></a> structure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师关闭地铁隧道，检查地下结构。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=underground&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>underground</strong></a> tunnel connects two subway lines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新地下隧道连接两条地铁线路。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an underground line</span> · 地铁线路</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=metro&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">metro</a></span> · 地铁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ventilation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ventilation</a></span> · 通风</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers closed the subway <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tunnel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tunnel</strong></a> to inspect the underground structure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师关闭地铁隧道，检查地下结构。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-108">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-108" class="vocabulary-word-title">rail<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-108" aria-label="定位 rail">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-108" data-mark-label="rail" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rail：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rail：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rail" title="在有道查询 rail">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-108</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n栏杆；铁路路轨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The railroad company restored a steam locomotive and replaced a damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rail</strong></a> on the heritage railway.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">铁路公司修复了一台蒸汽机车，并更换了观光铁路上的一根损坏钢轨。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The railway museum displays a locomotive, a section of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rail</strong></a> and an old railroad company sign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">铁路博物馆展出机车、一段钢轨和一块旧铁路公司标牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rail</strong></a> stopped the locomotive, forcing the railroad operator to close the railway temporarily.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一根断轨使机车停下，迫使铁路运营商暂时关闭铁路。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a steel rail</span> · 钢轨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=track&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">track</a></span> · 轨道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rail%20network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rail network</a></span> · 铁路网</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 14.09｜出发、抵达、延误与速度 {#group-14-09}

**串联方式：** 用起终点描述行程，再解释延期、暂歇、到期和应到时间，并补充速度。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">depart<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-109" aria-label="定位 depart">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-109" data-mark-label="depart" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="depart：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="depart：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depart&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 depart" title="在有道查询 depart">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 离开，启程</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depart&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depart</strong></a> at noon and are due to arrive at our destination before sunset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们中午出发，预计日落前到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The train is due to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depart&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depart</strong></a> soon and should arrive at its destination on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">火车即将出发，应该能准时到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers asked when they would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=depart&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>depart</strong></a> and whether they were still due to arrive at the original destination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客询问何时出发，以及是否仍按计划到达原定目的地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">depart at noon</span> · 中午出发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">arrival</a></span> · 到达</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=departure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">departure</a></span> · 出发</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">arrive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-110" aria-label="定位 arrive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-110" data-mark-label="arrive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arrive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arrive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arrive" title="在有道查询 arrive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 到达；取得成功</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We depart at noon and are due to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrive</strong></a> at our destination before sunset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们中午出发，预计日落前到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The train is due to depart soon and should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrive</strong></a> at its destination on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">火车即将出发，应该能准时到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers asked when they would depart and whether they were still due to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrive</strong></a> at the original destination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客询问何时出发，以及是否仍按计划到达原定目的地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">arrive on time</span> · 准时抵达</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">arrival</a></span> · 到达</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=departure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">departure</a></span> · 出发</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">destination<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-111" aria-label="定位 destination">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-111" data-mark-label="destination" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="destination：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="destination：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 destination" title="在有道查询 destination">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-111</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We depart at noon and are due to arrive at our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destination</strong></a> before sunset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们中午出发，预计日落前到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The train is due to depart soon and should arrive at its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destination</strong></a> on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">火车即将出发，应该能准时到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers asked when they would depart and whether they were still due to arrive at the original <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destination</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客询问何时出发，以及是否仍按计划到达原定目的地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a final destination</span> · 最终目的地</p>
