<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">delay<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-112" aria-label="定位 delay">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-112" data-mark-label="delay" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="delay：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="delay：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 delay" title="在有道查询 delay">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 耽搁；延期；v. 使耽搁；使延期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delay</strong></a> forced us to postpone the trip and defer the hotel payment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">延误迫使我们推迟旅行，并延后酒店付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may defer our decision, but a further <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delay</strong></a> could make us postpone the entire tour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以延后决定，但进一步耽搁可能让我们推迟整个行程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than postpone everything, we will defer one visit to avoid a longer <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delay</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将延后一次参观，避免更长耽搁，而非推迟所有安排。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a long delay</span> · 长时间延误</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reschedule&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reschedule</a></span> · 重新安排</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disruption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disruption</a></span> · 中断</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">postpone<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-113" aria-label="定位 postpone">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-113" data-mark-label="postpone" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="postpone：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="postpone：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postpone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 postpone" title="在有道查询 postpone">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 推迟，使延期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The delay forced us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postpone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postpone</strong></a> the trip and defer the hotel payment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">延误迫使我们推迟旅行，并延后酒店付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may defer our decision, but a further delay could make us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postpone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postpone</strong></a> the entire tour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以延后决定，但进一步耽搁可能让我们推迟整个行程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postpone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postpone</strong></a> everything, we will defer one visit to avoid a longer delay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将延后一次参观，避免更长耽搁，而非推迟所有安排。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">postpone a trip</span> · 推迟旅行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reschedule&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reschedule</a></span> · 重新安排</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disruption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disruption</a></span> · 中断</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">defer<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-114" aria-label="定位 defer">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-114" data-mark-label="defer" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="defer：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="defer：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 defer" title="在有道查询 defer">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 推迟，拖延</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The delay forced us to postpone the trip and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defer</strong></a> the hotel payment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">延误迫使我们推迟旅行，并延后酒店付款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defer</strong></a> our decision, but a further delay could make us postpone the entire tour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以延后决定，但进一步耽搁可能让我们推迟整个行程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than postpone everything, we will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>defer</strong></a> one visit to avoid a longer delay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将延后一次参观，避免更长耽搁，而非推迟所有安排。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">defer a decision</span> · 延后决定</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reschedule&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reschedule</a></span> · 重新安排</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disruption&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disruption</a></span> · 中断</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-115">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-115" class="vocabulary-word-title">lull<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-115" aria-label="定位 lull">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-115" data-mark-label="lull" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lull：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lull：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lull&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lull" title="在有道查询 lull">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-115</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 暂停，暂时平静期；v. 使昏昏欲睡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lull&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lull</strong></a> in bookings, the agent noticed that our travel vouchers would soon expire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预订的短暂淡季里，代理发现我们的旅行代金券即将过期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lull&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lull</strong></a> between departures to check when your ticket will expire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">利用发车间隙，检查车票何时过期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lull&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lull</strong></a> in tourist traffic gave the office time to renew permits before they could expire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客流量的短暂回落让办公室有时间在许可证到期前办理续期。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lull in activity</span> · 活动的短暂平静</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=validity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">validity</a></span> · 有效期</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=renewal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">renewal</a></span> · 续期</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">expire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-116" aria-label="定位 expire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-116" data-mark-label="expire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="expire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="expire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 expire" title="在有道查询 expire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 期满；气绝，死亡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During a lull in bookings, the agent noticed that our travel vouchers would soon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">预订的短暂淡季里，代理发现我们的旅行代金券即将过期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the lull between departures to check when your ticket will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">利用发车间隙，检查车票何时过期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A lull in tourist traffic gave the office time to renew permits before they could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游客流量的短暂回落让办公室有时间在许可证到期前办理续期。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a ticket will expire</span> · 车票将过期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=validity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">validity</a></span> · 有效期</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=renewal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">renewal</a></span> · 续期</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">due<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-117" aria-label="定位 due">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-117" data-mark-label="due" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="due：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="due：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 due" title="在有道查询 due">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.到期的，预期的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We depart at noon and are <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>due</strong></a> to arrive at our destination before sunset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们中午出发，预计日落前到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The train is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>due</strong></a> to depart soon and should arrive at its destination on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">火车即将出发，应该能准时到达目的地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Passengers asked when they would depart and whether they were still <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>due</strong></a> to arrive at the original destination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乘客询问何时出发，以及是否仍按计划到达原定目的地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">due to arrive</span> · 预计到达</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">arrival</a></span> · 到达</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=departure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">departure</a></span> · 出发</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-132">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-132" class="vocabulary-word-title">speed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-132" aria-label="定位 speed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-132" data-mark-label="speed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="speed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="speed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 speed" title="在有道查询 speed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-132</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.速度；迅速</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swift boat maintained its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>speed</strong></a> despite changes in wind velocity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这艘快艇虽遇风速变化，仍保持了速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swift response of the pilot reduced the aircraft’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>speed</strong></a> as wind velocity increased.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风速上升时，飞行员迅速反应，降低了飞机速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sensors measure <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=speed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>speed</strong></a> and wind velocity to ensure a swift but safe crossing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">传感器测量行驶速度和风速，确保快速而安全地渡过。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">average speed</span> · 平均速度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acceleration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">acceleration</a></span> · 加速度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">velocity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-133" aria-label="定位 velocity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-133" data-mark-label="velocity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="velocity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="velocity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velocity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 velocity" title="在有道查询 velocity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.速度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swift boat maintained its speed despite changes in wind <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velocity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>velocity</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这艘快艇虽遇风速变化，仍保持了速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swift response of the pilot reduced the aircraft’s speed as wind <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velocity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>velocity</strong></a> increased.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风速上升时，飞行员迅速反应，降低了飞机速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sensors measure speed and wind <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velocity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>velocity</strong></a> to ensure a swift but safe crossing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">传感器测量行驶速度和风速，确保快速而安全地渡过。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wind velocity</span> · 风速</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acceleration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">acceleration</a></span> · 加速度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-134">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-134" class="vocabulary-word-title">swift<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-134" aria-label="定位 swift">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-134" data-mark-label="swift" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swift：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swift：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swift" title="在有道查询 swift">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-134</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.迅速的；敏捷的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swift</strong></a> boat maintained its speed despite changes in wind velocity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这艘快艇虽遇风速变化，仍保持了速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swift</strong></a> response of the pilot reduced the aircraft’s speed as wind velocity increased.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风速上升时，飞行员迅速反应，降低了飞机速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sensors measure speed and wind velocity to ensure a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swift</strong></a> but safe crossing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">传感器测量行驶速度和风速，确保快速而安全地渡过。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a swift response</span> · 迅速反应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acceleration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">acceleration</a></span> · 加速度</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 14.10｜包裹怎样送到目的地 {#group-14-10}

**串联方式：** 由邮资、邮票和包装进入捆扎、装载、转运、传送与递送。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-118">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-118" class="vocabulary-word-title">postage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-118" aria-label="定位 postage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-118" data-mark-label="postage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="postage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="postage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 postage" title="在有道查询 postage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-118</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n邮费，邮资</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Attach a stamp with sufficient <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postage</strong></a> before you mail the packet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">邮寄小包裹前，贴上邮资足够的邮票。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The packet entered the mail system without a stamp, so the recipient had to pay the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小包裹没贴邮票就进入邮政系统，因此收件人不得不付邮费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ask how much <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=postage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>postage</strong></a> the packet needs and buy a stamp before you mail it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先问小包裹需要多少邮资，买好邮票再寄。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pay postage</span> · 付邮费</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=post%20office&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">post office</a></span> · 邮局</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recipient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recipient</a></span> · 收件人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">package<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-122" aria-label="定位 package">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-122" data-mark-label="package" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="package：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="package：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=package&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 package" title="在有道查询 package">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 包裹;［美］盒，包；v. 包装</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bind the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=package&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>package</strong></a> with string before sending the parcel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄包裹前，用绳捆好包装。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The courier asked us to bind the parcel more securely and label the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=package&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>package</strong></a> clearly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">快递员让我们把包裹捆得更牢，并清楚标注包装。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use strong tape to bind the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=package&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>package</strong></a> so the parcel will not open in transit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用结实胶带封牢包装，避免包裹运输中打开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wrap a package</span> · 包好包裹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=packaging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">packaging</a></span> · 包装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=courier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">courier</a></span> · 快递员</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">bind<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-123" aria-label="定位 bind">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-123" data-mark-label="bind" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bind：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bind：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bind" title="在有道查询 bind">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 捆，绑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Bind</strong></a> the package with string before sending the parcel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">寄包裹前，用绳捆好包装。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The courier asked us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bind</strong></a> the parcel more securely and label the package clearly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">快递员让我们把包裹捆得更牢，并清楚标注包装。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use strong tape to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bind</strong></a> the package so the parcel will not open in transit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用结实胶带封牢包装，避免包裹运输中打开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bind with string</span> · 用绳捆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=packaging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">packaging</a></span> · 包装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=courier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">courier</a></span> · 快递员</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-125">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We will transfer the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>load</strong></a> to a larger truck to reduce the burden on the smaller vehicle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将把货物转到更大卡车，减轻小车负担。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The crew must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>load</strong></a> carefully before the transfer, since excess weight places a burden on the axle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由于超重会加重车轴负担，船员转运前必须谨慎装载。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A transfer of the heavy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>load</strong></a> removed a costly burden from the ageing delivery van.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">转走重货减轻了老旧送货厢式车的昂贵负担。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">load a truck</span> · 给卡车装货</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=payload&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">payload</a></span> · 有效载荷</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logistics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logistics</a></span> · 物流</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-126">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We will transfer the load to a larger truck to reduce the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burden&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burden</strong></a> on the smaller vehicle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将把货物转到更大卡车，减轻小车负担。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A transfer of the heavy load removed a costly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burden&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burden</strong></a> from the ageing delivery van.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">转走重货减轻了老旧送货厢式车的昂贵负担。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="14-128">
<summary><h4 id="word-14-128" class="vocabulary-word-title">transmit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-14-128" aria-label="定位 transmit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="14-128" data-mark-label="transmit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="transmit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="transmit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transmit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 transmit" title="在有道查询 transmit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">14-128</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 传送；传播，传染；传输</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">While trucks convey goods in transit, tracking devices <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transmit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transmit</strong></a> updates so couriers can deliver on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卡车运送在途货物时，追踪设备传输更新，帮助快递员准时递送。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The system will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transmit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transmit</strong></a> a warning if vehicles that convey medicines face a transit delay and cannot deliver them safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果运药车辆途中延误、无法安全送达，系统将传输警报。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We use vans to convey parcels, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transmit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transmit</strong></a> location data during transit and deliver each item to the correct address.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用厢式车运包裹，运输途中传输位置数据，并将每件物品送到正确地址。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">transmit information</span> · 传输信息</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distribution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">distribution</a></span> · 配送</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">While trucks convey goods in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transit</strong></a>, tracking devices transmit updates so couriers can deliver on time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卡车运送在途货物时，追踪设备传输更新，帮助快递员准时递送。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The system will transmit a warning if vehicles that convey medicines face a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transit</strong></a> delay and cannot deliver them safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果运药车辆途中延误、无法安全送达，系统将传输警报。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We use vans to convey parcels, transmit location data during <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>transit</strong></a> and deliver each item to the correct address.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用厢式车运包裹，运输途中传输位置数据，并将每件物品送到正确地址。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distribution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">distribution</a></span> · 配送</li></ul>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **plan an itinerary**：规划行程；plan 为补充词。
- **reach a destination**：抵达目的地；reach 为补充词。
- **a delayed flight**：延误的航班；delayed 来自 delay。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

Before a journey, travellers can check their visa, plan a route and compare fares. They should also consider baggage limits and the time needed for transit. A flight or train may be delayed, so a flexible itinerary can help. On arrival, passengers collect their luggage and continue to their destination.

**中文理解：** 出发前，旅行者可以检查签证、规划路线并比较票价，也应考虑行李限额和转乘时间。航班或列车可能延误，因此灵活的行程安排会有帮助。抵达后，乘客领取行李并继续前往目的地。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 讲清一次旅行：准备、路线、交通、行李、转乘、延误和抵达。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：交通旅行
我记得的词群：
每群想起的词或搭配：
今天的 3 个 takeaway：
我的 3—5 句英文：
看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```
